  * Package: Includes 40 pcs disco balls. Diameter 5 cm / 2 inches (Motor and Lamps are not included). 
  * The rotating mirror ball reflects the light on its surface. With hanging string, these mini disco balls can be hung on trees and reflect bright lights. 
  * The mirror balls are ideal for centerpiece, cake decorations, musical festivals, birthday parties, Christmas, dance floors etc. Ideal for parties and home discos. 
  * Applications: KTV, Home party, Clubbing, Stage, etc. These mini mirror balls will be a great addition to your party decorations. 
  * The silver disco balls are covered with shiny mirror squares, they spark beautifully under lights reflection. In this way, splendid light effect will be created.

## Product description
![COMPANY LOGO](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)
###  Suwimut 40 Pack Mirror Disco Ball, 2 Inch Silver Hanging Disco Light Mirror Ball with Attached String for Party Wedding Home Decoration, Stage Props 
![S-DiscoBall-2in-40pc.PT01](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)
Suwimut 40PCS Hanging Disco Ball is the perfect Christmas tree ornament, measuring 2" with string for decoration, this design will never go out of style. Shine different colored lights towards the glass mirror ball to produce a spectacular mix of colored spots all over the room. 
![S-DiscoBall-2in-40pc.PT02](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)
Package includes 40 pcs disco balls. Diameter 5 cm / 2 inches (Motor and Lamps are not included). 
![S-DiscoBall-2in-40pc.PT03](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)
The silver disco balls are covered with shiny mirror squares, they spark beautifully under lights reflection. In this way, splendid light effect will be created. 
![S-DiscoBall-2in-40pc.PT04](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)
The mirror balls are ideal for centerpiece, cake decorations, musical festivals, birthday parties, Christmas, dance floors etc. Ideal for parties and home discos.

## About this Item
  * Package: Includes 40 pcs disco balls. Diameter 5 cm / 2 inches (Motor and Lamps are not included).
  * The rotating mirror ball reflects the light on its surface. With hanging string, these mini disco balls can be hung on trees and reflect bright lights.
  * The mirror balls are ideal for centerpiece, cake decorations, musical festivals, birthday parties, Christmas, dance floors etc. Ideal for parties and home discos.
  * Applications: KTV, Home party, Clubbing, Stage, etc. These mini mirror balls will be a great addition to your party decorations.
  * The silver disco balls are covered with shiny mirror squares, they spark beautifully under lights reflection. In this way, splendid light effect will be created.


## Product Description
Suwimut 40PCS Hanging Disco Ball is the perfect Christmas tree ornament, measuring 2" with string for decoration, this design will never go out of style. Shine different colored lights towards the glass mirror ball to produce a spectacular mix of colored spots all over the room.
